Recipe: Cake
Ingredients:
Flour - 2 cups
Sugar - 1 cup
Eggs - 2
Milk - 1 cup
Butter - 100 grams
Baking Powder - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Preheat the oven to 180 degrees Celsius.
2. Mix flour, sugar and baking powder in a bowl.
3. Add eggs, milk and melted butter.
4. Mix all the ingredients until the batter becomes smooth.
5. Pour the batter into a greased baking pan.
6. Bake for 30 to 35 minutes.
7. Allow the cake to cool before serving.

Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 35 minutes

Recipe: Pasta
Ingredients:
Pasta - 200 grams
Tomato - 2
Onion - 1
Garlic - 3 cloves
Olive Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Boil the pasta in water until it becomes soft.
2. Chop the tomato, onion and garlic.
3. Heat olive oil in a pan.
4. Add onion and garlic and cook for a few minutes.
5. Add chopped tomatoes and salt.
6. Add the cooked pasta and mix well.
7. Serve the pasta hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 20 minutes

Recipe: Pizza
Ingredients:
Pizza Base - 1
Tomato Sauce - 4 tablespoons
Mozzarella Cheese - 1 cup
Onion - 1
Capsicum - 1
Oregano - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Place the pizza base on a baking tray.
2. Spread tomato sauce over the base.
3. Add mozzarella cheese.
4. Add chopped onion and capsicum.
5. Sprinkle oregano on top.
6. Bake the pizza until the cheese melts and the base becomes crispy.
7. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 20 minutes

Recipe: Fried Rice
Ingredients:
Rice - 2 cups
Carrot - 1
Beans - 1 cup
Capsicum - 1
Onion - 1
Soy Sauce - 2 tablespoons
Oil - 2 tablespoons

Instructions:
1. Cook the rice and allow it to cool.
2. Chop all the vegetables.
3. Heat oil in a pan.
4. Add onion and vegetables and stir-fry them.
5. Add the cooked rice.
6. Add soy sauce and mix everything well.
7. Cook for a few minutes and serve hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 15 minutes

Recipe: Biryani
Ingredients:
Basmati Rice - 2 cups
Chicken - 500 grams
Onion - 2
Tomato - 2
Ginger Garlic Paste - 2 tablespoons
Biryani Masala - 2 tablespoons
Oil - 3 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Wash and soak the basmati rice.
2. Heat oil in a large cooking pot.
3. Add sliced onions and cook until golden brown.
4. Add ginger garlic paste and tomatoes.
5. Add chicken, salt and biryani masala.
6. Cook the chicken until it becomes tender.
7. Add the rice and sufficient water.
8. Cook until the rice is completely done.
9. Serve the biryani hot.

Preparation Time: 30 minutes
Cooking Time: 45 minutes

Recipe: Pancakes
Ingredients:
Flour - 1 cup
Milk - 1 cup
Egg - 1
Sugar - 2 tablespoons
Butter - 2 tablespoons
Baking Powder - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Mix flour, sugar and baking powder in a bowl.
2. Add milk and egg to the mixture.
3. Whisk until the batter becomes smooth.
4. Heat a pan and add a small amount of butter.
5. Pour the batter onto the pan.
6. Cook both sides until golden brown.
7. Serve the pancakes warm.

Preparation Time: 10 minutes
Cooking Time: 15 minutes

Recipe: Sandwich
Ingredients:
Bread - 4 slices
Cheese - 2 slices
Tomato - 1
Onion - 1
Capsicum - 1
Butter - 1 tablespoon

Instructions:
1. Slice the tomato, onion and capsicum.
2. Spread butter on the bread slices.
3. Place vegetables and cheese between the bread slices.
4. Heat a pan or sandwich maker.
5. Toast the sandwich until golden brown.
6. Cut the sandwich into two pieces.
7. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 10 minutes
Cooking Time: 10 minutes

Recipe: Vegetable Soup
Ingredients:
Carrot - 1
Beans - 1 cup
Cabbage - 1 cup
Onion - 1
Garlic - 2 cloves
Water - 3 cups
Salt - 1 teaspoon
Pepper - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Wash and chop all the vegetables.
2. Heat a little oil in a cooking pot.
3. Add onion and garlic and cook for a few minutes.
4. Add the vegetables and water.
5. Add salt and pepper.
6. Cook until the vegetables become soft.
7. Serve the soup hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Chocolate Brownie
Ingredients:
All-Purpose Flour - 1 cup
Cocoa Powder - 1/2 cup
Sugar - 1 cup
Eggs - 2
Butter - 100 grams
Milk - 1/2 cup
Vanilla Essence - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Preheat the oven to 180 degrees Celsius.
2. Melt the butter and allow it to cool slightly.
3. Mix flour and cocoa powder in a bowl.
4. Add sugar, eggs, milk and vanilla essence.
5. Mix until the batter becomes smooth.
6. Pour the mixture into a baking tray.
7. Bake for 25 to 30 minutes.
8. Allow the brownie to cool before serving.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 30 minutes

Recipe: Vegetable Noodles
Ingredients:
Noodles - 200 grams
Carrot - 1
Capsicum - 1
Cabbage - 1 cup
Onion - 1
Soy Sauce - 2 tablespoons
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Boil the noodles according to the package instructions.
2. Chop all the vegetables.
3. Heat oil in a pan.
4. Add onion and vegetables and stir-fry them.
5. Add the cooked noodles.
6. Add soy sauce and salt.
7. Mix everything well and cook for a few minutes.
8. Serve the noodles hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 15 minutes

Recipe: Samosa
Ingredients:
All-Purpose Flour - 2 cups
Potatoes - 3
Green Peas - 1/2 cup
Onion - 1
Green Chilli - 1
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Oil - 2 cups
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Prepare dough using flour, water and salt.
2. Boil and mash the potatoes.
3. Cook potatoes, peas, onion and spices together.
4. Divide the dough into small portions.
5. Roll each portion and fill it with the potato mixture.
6. Fold and seal the edges properly.
7. Deep fry the samosas until golden brown.
8. Serve hot with chutney.

Preparation Time: 30 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Masala Dosa
Ingredients:
Dosa Rice - 2 cups
Urad Dal - 1 cup
Potatoes - 4
Onion - 2
Green Chilli - 2
Mustard Seeds - 1 teaspoon
Oil - 3 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Soak rice and urad dal separately for several hours.
2. Grind them into a smooth batter and ferment overnight.
3. Boil and mash the potatoes.
4. Cook onion, green chilli and mustard seeds in oil.
5. Add the mashed potatoes and salt.
6. Heat a dosa pan and spread the batter into a thin circle.
7. Add the potato filling and fold the dosa.
8. Serve hot with chutney and sambar.

Preparation Time: 30 minutes
Cooking Time: 20 minutes

Recipe: Idli
Ingredients:
Idli Rice - 2 cups
Urad Dal - 1 cup
Salt - 1 teaspoon
Water - 3 cups

Instructions:
1. Soak rice and urad dal separately.
2. Grind them into a smooth batter.
3. Mix the batter and allow it to ferment overnight.
4. Add salt and mix well.
5. Grease the idli moulds.
6. Pour the batter into the moulds.
7. Steam the idlis for about 10 to 12 minutes.
8. Serve hot with chutney and sambar.

Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 15 minutes

Recipe: Chicken Curry
Ingredients:
Chicken - 500 grams
Onion - 2
Tomato - 2
Ginger Garlic Paste - 2 tablespoons
Turmeric Powder - 1 teaspoon
Chilli Powder - 1 teaspoon
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Oil - 3 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Clean the chicken and keep it aside.
2. Heat oil in a pan.
3. Add onions and cook until golden brown.
4. Add ginger garlic paste and tomatoes.
5. Add turmeric, chilli powder and salt.
6. Add chicken and mix well.
7. Cover and cook until the chicken becomes tender.
8. Add garam masala and cook for a few more minutes.
9. Serve hot with rice or roti.

Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 35 minutes

Recipe: Paneer Butter Masala
Ingredients:
Paneer - 250 grams
Tomato - 3
Onion - 1
Butter - 2 tablespoons
Cream - 1/2 cup
Ginger Garlic Paste - 1 tablespoon
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Cut the paneer into small cubes.
2. Heat butter in a pan.
3. Add onion and ginger garlic paste.
4. Add chopped tomatoes and cook until soft.
5. Blend the mixture into a smooth paste.
6. Add the paste back to the pan with salt and garam masala.
7. Add paneer and cream.
8. Cook for a few minutes and serve hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Chole
Ingredients:
Chickpeas - 2 cups
Onion - 2
Tomato - 2
Ginger Garlic Paste - 1 tablespoon
Chole Masala - 2 teaspoons
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Soak chickpeas overnight.
2. Boil the chickpeas until soft.
3. Heat oil in a pan.
4. Add onions and cook until golden brown.
5. Add ginger garlic paste and tomatoes.
6. Add chole masala and salt.
7. Add boiled chickpeas and mix well.
8. Cook for 10 minutes and serve hot.

Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 30 minutes

Recipe: Aloo Paratha
Ingredients:
Wheat Flour - 2 cups
Potatoes - 3
Onion - 1
Green Chilli - 1
Coriander - 2 tablespoons
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Prepare dough using wheat flour, water and salt.
2. Boil and mash the potatoes.
3. Add onion, chilli, coriander and garam masala to the potatoes.
4. Divide the dough into small portions.
5. Fill each portion with the potato mixture.
6. Roll into a round paratha.
7. Cook on a hot pan using a little oil.
8. Serve hot with curd or pickle.

Preparation Time: 25 minutes
Cooking Time: 20 minutes

Recipe: Vegetable Fried Noodles
Ingredients:
Noodles - 200 grams
Carrot - 1
Capsicum - 1
Cabbage - 1 cup
Onion - 1
Soy Sauce - 2 tablespoons
Chilli Sauce - 1 tablespoon
Oil - 2 tablespoons

Instructions:
1. Boil the noodles until soft and drain the water.
2. Chop all the vegetables.
3. Heat oil in a wok or pan.
4. Add onion and vegetables and stir-fry.
5. Add soy sauce and chilli sauce.
6. Add the cooked noodles.
7. Mix everything well and cook for a few minutes.
8. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 15 minutes

Recipe: Tomato Soup
Ingredients:
Tomato - 5
Onion - 1
Garlic - 2 cloves
Water - 3 cups
Butter - 1 tablespoon
Pepper - 1 teaspoon
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Wash and chop the tomatoes and onion.
2. Heat butter in a cooking pot.
3. Add onion and garlic and cook for a few minutes.
4. Add tomatoes and water.
5. Cook until the tomatoes become soft.
6. Blend the mixture until smooth.
7. Add salt and pepper.
8. Simmer for a few minutes and serve hot.

Preparation Time: 10 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: French Toast
Ingredients:
Bread - 4 slices
Eggs - 2
Milk - 1/2 cup
Sugar - 2 tablespoons
Butter - 1 tablespoon
Vanilla Essence - 1/2 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Beat eggs, milk, sugar and vanilla essence in a bowl.
2. Dip each bread slice into the mixture.
3. Heat butter in a pan.
4. Place the bread slices on the pan.
5. Cook both sides until golden brown.
6. Remove from the pan.
7. Serve warm.

Preparation Time: 5 minutes
Cooking Time: 10 minutes

Recipe: Omelette
Ingredients:
Eggs - 2
Onion - 1
Tomato - 1
Green Chilli - 1
Pepper - 1/2 teaspoon
Salt - 1/2 teaspoon
Oil - 1 tablespoon

Instructions:
1. Crack the eggs into a bowl.
2. Add chopped onion, tomato and green chilli.
3. Add salt and pepper.
4. Beat the mixture well.
5. Heat oil in a pan.
6. Pour the egg mixture into the pan.
7. Cook both sides until done.
8. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 5 minutes
Cooking Time: 10 minutes

Recipe: Mango Smoothie
Ingredients:
Mango - 1
Milk - 1 cup
Yogurt - 1/2 cup
Sugar - 1 tablespoon
Ice Cubes - 4

Instructions:
1. Peel and chop the mango.
2. Add mango, milk and yogurt to a blender.
3. Add sugar and ice cubes.
4. Blend until smooth.
5. Pour into a glass.
6. Serve chilled.

Preparation Time: 5 minutes
Cooking Time: 5 minutes

Recipe: Fruit Salad
Ingredients:
Apple - 1
Banana - 1
Mango - 1
Orange - 1
Grapes - 1 cup
Honey - 1 tablespoon
Lemon Juice - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Wash and chop all the fruits.
2. Place the fruits in a large bowl.
3. Add honey and lemon juice.
4. Mix gently.
5. Chill for a few minutes.
6. Serve fresh.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 5 minutes

Recipe: Gulab Jamun
Ingredients:
Milk Powder - 1 cup
All-Purpose Flour - 2 tablespoons
Milk - 1/4 cup
Sugar - 2 cups
Water - 2 cups
Cardamom Powder - 1/2 teaspoon
Oil - 2 cups

Instructions:
1. Mix milk powder and flour in a bowl.
2. Add milk and prepare a soft dough.
3. Divide the dough into small balls.
4. Heat oil in a pan.
5. Fry the balls until golden brown.
6. Prepare sugar syrup using sugar and water.
7. Add cardamom powder to the syrup.
8. Soak the fried balls in the warm syrup.
9. Serve warm.

Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Kheer
Ingredients:
Rice - 1/2 cup
Milk - 1 litre
Sugar - 1/2 cup
Cardamom Powder - 1/2 teaspoon
Cashews - 2 tablespoons
Raisins - 2 tablespoons

Instructions:
1. Wash the rice and drain the water.
2. Boil milk in a heavy-bottomed pan.
3. Add rice and cook on low heat.
4. Stir regularly until the rice becomes soft.
5. Add sugar and cardamom powder.
6. Add cashews and raisins.
7. Cook for a few more minutes.
8. Serve warm or chilled.

Preparation Time: 10 minutes
Cooking Time: 40 minutes

Recipe: Chocolate Mug Cake
Ingredients:
All-Purpose Flour - 4 tablespoons
Cocoa Powder - 2 tablespoons
Sugar - 2 tablespoons
Milk - 4 tablespoons
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Baking Powder - 1/2 teaspoon
Chocolate Chips - 1 tablespoon

Instructions:
1. Add flour, cocoa powder, sugar and baking powder to a mug.
2. Add milk and oil.
3. Mix until the batter becomes smooth.
4. Add chocolate chips.
5. Microwave for about 1 to 2 minutes.
6. Allow the cake to cool slightly.
7. Serve warm.

Preparation Time: 5 minutes
Cooking Time: 2 minutes

Recipe: Macaroni
Ingredients:
Macaroni - 200 grams
Onion - 1
Tomato - 2
Capsicum - 1
Tomato Sauce - 3 tablespoons
Cheese - 1/2 cup
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Boil the macaroni until soft.
2. Drain the water and keep it aside.
3. Heat oil in a pan.
4. Add onion, tomato and capsicum.
5. Add tomato sauce and salt.
6. Add the cooked macaroni.
7. Mix well and add cheese.
8. Cook until the cheese melts.
9. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 10 minutes
Cooking Time: 20 minutes

Recipe: Manchurian
Ingredients:
Cabbage - 1 cup
Carrot - 1
Capsicum - 1
All-Purpose Flour - 1/2 cup
Corn Flour - 2 tablespoons
Soy Sauce - 2 tablespoons
Chilli Sauce - 1 tablespoon
Garlic - 3 cloves
Oil - 2 cups

Instructions:
1. Chop cabbage, carrot and capsicum.
2. Mix the vegetables with flour and corn flour.
3. Prepare small balls from the mixture.
4. Deep fry the balls until golden brown.
5. Heat a little oil in a pan.
6. Add garlic, soy sauce and chilli sauce.
7. Add the fried balls and mix well.
8. Cook for a few minutes and serve hot.

Preparation Time: 25 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Tandoori Chicken
Ingredients:
Chicken - 500 grams
Yogurt - 1 cup
Ginger Garlic Paste - 2 tablespoons
Red Chilli Powder - 1 teaspoon
Turmeric Powder - 1/2 teaspoon
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Lemon Juice - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Clean the chicken and make small cuts.
2. Mix yogurt, ginger garlic paste and spices.
3. Add lemon juice and salt.
4. Coat the chicken with the marinade.
5. Marinate for at least 2 hours.
6. Preheat the oven to 200 degrees Celsius.
7. Place the chicken on a baking tray.
8. Bake until the chicken is fully cooked and slightly charred.
9. Serve hot.

Preparation Time: 2 hours
Cooking Time: 40 minutes

Recipe: Palak Paneer
Ingredients:
Paneer - 250 grams
Spinach - 300 grams
Onion - 1
Tomato - 1
Ginger Garlic Paste - 1 tablespoon
Garam Masala - 1 teaspoon
Cream - 2 tablespoons
Oil - 2 tablespoons
Salt - 1 teaspoon

Instructions:
1. Wash and blanch the spinach.
2. Blend the spinach into a smooth puree.
3. Heat oil in a pan.
4. Add onion and ginger garlic paste.
5. Add chopped tomato and cook until soft.
6. Add spinach puree and salt.
7. Add paneer cubes and garam masala.
8. Add cream and cook for a few minutes.
9. Serve hot with roti or rice.

Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes

Recipe: Classic Kofta
Ingredients:
Rice - 2 cups
Onion - 1
Carrot - 1
Peas - 1 cup
Spices - As required
Preparation Time: 31 minutes

Onion - 1
Tomato - 2
Garlic - 3 cloves
Ginger - 1 inch
Spices - As required
Preparation Time: 15 minutes
Cooking Time: 25 minutes
Instructions:
1. Prepare and measure all ingredients.
2. Heat oil or butter in a suitable pan.
3. Add the main ingredients and cook until tender.
4. Add spices and season to taste.
5. Cook thoroughly and serve hot.

Rice - 2 cups
Onion - 1
Carrot - 1
Peas - 1 cup
Spices - As required
Preparation Time: 20 minutes
Cooking Time: 32 minutes
Instructions:
1. Prepare and measure all ingredients.
2. Heat oil or butter in a suitable pan.
3. Add the main ingredients and cook until tender.
4. Add spices and season to taste.
5. Cook thoroughly and serve hot.

Paneer - 250 grams
Tomato - 3
Onion - 1
Butter - 50 grams
Spices - As required
Preparation Time: 13 minutes
Cooking Time: 50 minutes
Instructions:
1. Prepare and measure all ingredients.
2. Heat oil or butter in a suitable pan.
3. Add the main ingredients and cook until tender.
4. Add spices and season to taste.
5. Cook thoroughly and serve hot.
